          Alert description

          Content Security Policy (CSP) is an added layer of security that helps to detect and mitigate certain types of attacks, including Cross Site Scripting (XSS) and data injection attacks. These attacks are used for everything from data theft to site defacement or distribution of malware. CSP provides a set of standard HTTP headers that allow website owners to declare approved sources of content that browsers should be allowed to load on that page — covered types are JavaScript, CSS, HTML frames, fonts, images and embeddable objects such as Java applets, ActiveX, audio and video files.

		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload','infinite-scroll' ]).
		controller('pixiProfileController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('/pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//console.log($scope.pictures);
				});
			}


			$scope.user_info = function(){
				$http.get('/user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})		
			}

			$scope.update_user_info = function(user){
				console.log($scope.user_upd.email);
				console.log(user);
				$http({
					method: 'PUT',
					url: '/user_info/' + user,
					data: { 
							'email': $scope.user_upd.email, 
	            			'password': $scope.user_upd.password, 
	            			'name': $scope.user_upd.name
	            		}
	            		}).then(function success(response){
							console.log('in success');
							$scope.user_alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Profile Updated " + ($scope.user_upd.email || '') + ' ' + ($scope.user_upd.password || '') + ' ' + ($scope.user_upd.name || '') },
			        		];
				        	$scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
					        	$scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
					    	}
			        	
						})	
				}
	


			$scope.user_delete_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('/user_delete_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){

						console.log('in delete');
						$scope.user_pictures();
						$scope.picture_alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Buh-Bye, Photo Deleted! " },
			        	];
		        	
				    	$scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
					})		
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('/user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('/user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})				
			}

			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('/user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
					})				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('/admin/total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})			
			}	

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('/admin/all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})				
			}

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)
				$http.get('/picture/' + picture_id + '/likes')
					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);
						$scope.individual_picture_likes = response.data;
					})			
			}

		});

          Parameter 
          x-frame-options
          Solution

          Modern Web browsers support the Content-Security-Policy and X-Frame-Options HTTP headers. Ensure one of them is set on all web pages returned by your site/app.

          If you expect the page to be framed only by pages on your server (e.g. it's part of a FRAMESET) then you'll want to use SAMEORIGIN, otherwise if you never expect the page to be framed, you should use DENY. Alternatively consider implementing Content Security Policy's "frame-ancestors" directive.

  4. Risk=Medium, Confidence=Low (1)

      1. Absence of Anti-CSRF Tokens (1)

          Alert tags

          • OWASP_2017_A05

          Alert description

          No Anti-CSRF tokens were found in a HTML submission form.

          A cross-site request forgery is an attack that involves forcing a victim to send an HTTP request to a target destination without their knowledge or intent in order to perform an action as the victim. The underlying cause is application functionality using predictable URL/form actions in a repeatable way. The nature of the attack is that CSRF exploits the trust that a web site has for a user. By contrast, cross-site scripting (XSS) exploits the trust that a user has for a web site. Like XSS, CSRF attacks are not necessarily cross-site, but they can be. Cross-site request forgery is also known as CSRF, XSRF, one-click attack, session riding, confused deputy, and sea surf.

          CSRF attacks are effective in a number of situations, including:

          * The victim has an active session on the target site.

          * The victim is authenticated via HTTP auth on the target site.

          * The victim is on the same local network as the target site.

          CSRF has primarily been used to perform an action against a target site using the victim's privileges, but recent techniques have been discovered to disclose information by gaining access to the response. The risk of information disclosure is dramatically increased when the target site is vulnerable to XSS, because XSS can be used as a platform for CSRF, allowing the attack to operate within the bounds of the same-origin policy.
          Other info

          No known Anti-CSRF token [anticsrf, CSRFToken, __RequestVerificationToken, csrfmiddlewaretoken, authenticity_token, OWASP_CSRFTOKEN, anoncsrf, csrf_token, _csrf, _csrfSecret, __csrf_magic, CSRF, _token, _csrf_token, _csrfToken] was found in the following HTML form: [Form 1: "email" "password" ].

          Solution

          Phase: Architecture and Design

          Use a vetted library or framework that does not allow this weakness to occur or provides constructs that make this weakness easier to avoid.

          For example, use anti-CSRF packages such as the OWASP CSRFGuard.

          Phase: Implementation

          Ensure that your application is free of cross-site scripting issues, because most CSRF defenses can be bypassed using attacker-controlled script.

          Phase: Architecture and Design

          Generate a unique nonce for each form, place the nonce into the form, and verify the nonce upon receipt of the form. Be sure that the nonce is not predictable (CWE-330).

          Note that this can be bypassed using XSS.

          Identify especially dangerous operations. When the user performs a dangerous operation, send a separate confirmation request to ensure that the user intended to perform that operation.

          Note that this can be bypassed using XSS.

          Use the ESAPI Session Management control.

          This control includes a component for CSRF.

          Do not use the GET method for any request that triggers a state change.

          Phase: Implementation

          Check the HTTP Referer header to see if the request originated from an expected page. This could break legitimate functionality, because users or proxies may have disabled sending the Referer for privacy reasons.

	<script>
		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload', 'infinite-scroll', 'ngMaterial', 'angularMoment' ]).
		config(function($sceProvider) {
			$sceProvider.enabled(false)
		}).
		controller('pixiController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
			}

			$scope.switch = function() {
				  	alert('hi');
				  	$scope = {
				    cb1: 'tdddddop',
				    cb1: 'ttt'
				  };

				  $scope.message = 'TOP 10';

				  $scope.onChange = function(cbState) {
				  	$scope.message = 'test';
				  };
			}

			$scope.upload_photo = function(file, error, next, $window) {
				console.log("inpicfunc" + $scope.upload.filename );
				if(!$scope.upload.filename) {
					console.log("no file found");
			   	
				} else {
					//console.log(file);
					Upload.upload({
	            	url: 'upload_photo/',
	            	method: 'POST',
	            	data: { 'file': $scope.upload.filename, 
	            			'title': $scope.upload.title, 
	            			'creator_id': $scope.user._id, 
	            			'username': $scope.user.email }
					}).then(function (resp) {
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.upload = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Photo Successfully Uploaded" },
			        	];
		        	
				    $scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
		        }, function (resp) {
		            console.log('Error status: ' + resp.status);
		        	});
				}
			}

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})
				
			}


			$scope.search = function(search_query){
				console.log($scope.search.query);
				$http.get('/search/?query=' + $scope.search.query)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
						$scope.search_response = null;
						$scope.clear_search = 1;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'success',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        ];
				        
				        }, function error(response) {
						$scope.search_response = response.data;
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'danger',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        	];
				       })
					}
				
			
			$scope.clear = function() {
				$scope.all_photos();
				$scope.clear_search = null;
				$scope.alerts = null;
			}

			$scope.user_info = function(){
				$http.get('user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})
				
			}
			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user_pictures = response.data;
					})
				
			}	
			$scope.like_photo = function(photo){
				console.log('in like');
				$http.get('like_photo/'+ photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}			
			$scope.love_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('love_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.load_more_photos = function(){
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
				
			}	

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)
				$http.get('picture/' + picture_id + '/likes')
					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);
						$scope.individual_picture_likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}
		});
	</script>

      6. X-Content-Type-Options Header Missing (1)
        1. GET http://localhost:8000/images/pixi_logo.png
          Alert tags
          • CWE-693
          • OWASP_2021_A05
          • OWASP_2017_A06
          • POLICY_QA_STD =
          • POLICY_PENTEST =
          • SYSTEMIC
          Alert description

          The Anti-MIME-Sniffing header X-Content-Type-Options was not set to 'nosniff'. This allows older versions of Internet Explorer and Chrome to perform MIME-sniffing on the response body, potentially causing the response body to be interpreted and displayed as a content type other than the declared content type. Current (early 2014) and legacy versions of Firefox will use the declared content type (if one is set), rather than performing MIME-sniffing.
          Other info

          This issue still applies to error type pages (401, 403, 500, etc.) as those pages are often still affected by injection issues, in which case there is still concern for browsers sniffing pages away from their actual content type.

          At "High" threshold this scan rule will not alert on client or server error responses.

		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload','infinite-scroll' ]).
		controller('pixiProfileController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('/pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;

				});
			}

				$http.get('/user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})		
			}

			$scope.update_user_info = function(user){
				console.log($scope.user_upd.email);
				console.log(user);
				$http({
					method: 'PUT',
					url: '/user_info/' + user,
					data: { 
							'email': $scope.user_upd.email, 
	            			'password': $scope.user_upd.password, 
	            			'name': $scope.user_upd.name
	            		}
	            		}).then(function success(response){
							console.log('in success');
							$scope.user_alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Profile Updated " + ($scope.user_upd.email || '') + ' ' + ($scope.user_upd.password || '') + ' ' + ($scope.user_upd.name || '') },
			        		];

					        	$scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
					    	}
			        	
						})	
				}
	


			$scope.user_delete_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('/user_delete_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){

						console.log('in delete');
						$scope.user_pictures();
						$scope.picture_alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Buh-Bye, Photo Deleted! " },
			        	];

				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
					})		
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('/user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('/user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})				
			}

			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('/user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
					})				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('/admin/total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})			
			}	

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('/admin/all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})				
			}

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)

					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);

					})

	<script>
		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload', 'infinite-scroll', 'ngMaterial', 'angularMoment' ]).
		config(function($sceProvider) {
			$sceProvider.enabled(false)
		}).
		controller('pixiController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
			}

			$scope.switch = function() {
				  	alert('hi');
				  	$scope = {
				    cb1: 'tdddddop',
				    cb1: 'ttt'
				  };

				  $scope.message = 'TOP 10';

				  $scope.onChange = function(cbState) {
				  	$scope.message = 'test';
				  };
			}

			$scope.upload_photo = function(file, error, next, $window) {
				console.log("inpicfunc" + $scope.upload.filename );
				if(!$scope.upload.filename) {
					console.log("no file found");
			   	
				} else {
					//console.log(file);
					Upload.upload({
	            	url: 'upload_photo/',
	            	method: 'POST',
	            	data: { 'file': $scope.upload.filename, 
	            			'title': $scope.upload.title, 
	            			'creator_id': $scope.user._id, 
	            			'username': $scope.user.email }
					}).then(function (resp) {
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.upload = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Photo Successfully Uploaded" },
			        	];
		        	
				    $scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
		        }, function (resp) {
		            console.log('Error status: ' + resp.status);
		        	});
				}
			}

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})
				
			}


			$scope.search = function(search_query){
				console.log($scope.search.query);
				$http.get('/search/?query=' + $scope.search.query)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
						$scope.search_response = null;
						$scope.clear_search = 1;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'success',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        ];
				        
				        }, function error(response) {
						$scope.search_response = response.data;
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'danger',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        	];
				       })
					}
				
			
			$scope.clear = function() {
				$scope.all_photos();
				$scope.clear_search = null;
				$scope.alerts = null;
			}

			$scope.user_info = function(){
				$http.get('user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})
				
			}
			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user_pictures = response.data;
					})
				
			}	
			$scope.like_photo = function(photo){
				console.log('in like');
				$http.get('like_photo/'+ photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}			
			$scope.love_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('love_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.load_more_photos = function(){
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
				
			}	

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)
				$http.get('picture/' + picture_id + '/likes')
					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);

	<script>
		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload', 'infinite-scroll', 'ngMaterial', 'angularMoment' ]).
		config(function($sceProvider) {
			$sceProvider.enabled(false)
		}).
		controller('pixiController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
			}

			$scope.switch = function() {
				  	alert('hi');
				  	$scope = {
				    cb1: 'tdddddop',
				    cb1: 'ttt'
				  };

				  $scope.message = 'TOP 10';

				  $scope.onChange = function(cbState) {
				  	$scope.message = 'test';
				  };
			}

			$scope.upload_photo = function(file, error, next, $window) {
				console.log("inpicfunc" + $scope.upload.filename );
				if(!$scope.upload.filename) {
					console.log("no file found");
			   	
				} else {
					//console.log(file);
					Upload.upload({
	            	url: 'upload_photo/',
	            	method: 'POST',
	            	data: { 'file': $scope.upload.filename, 
	            			'title': $scope.upload.title, 
	            			'creator_id': $scope.user._id, 
	            			'username': $scope.user.email }
					}).then(function (resp) {
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.upload = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Photo Successfully Uploaded" },
			        	];
		        	
				    $scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
		        }, function (resp) {
		            console.log('Error status: ' + resp.status);
		        	});
				}
			}

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})
				
			}


			$scope.search = function(search_query){
				console.log($scope.search.query);
				$http.get('/search/?query=' + $scope.search.query)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
						$scope.search_response = null;
						$scope.clear_search = 1;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'success',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        ];
				        
				        }, function error(response) {
						$scope.search_response = response.data;
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'danger',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        	];
				       })
					}
				
			
			$scope.clear = function() {
				$scope.all_photos();
				$scope.clear_search = null;
				$scope.alerts = null;
			}

			$scope.user_info = function(){
				$http.get('user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})
				
			}
			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user_pictures = response.data;
					})
				
			}	
			$scope.like_photo = function(photo){
				console.log('in like');
				$http.get('like_photo/'+ photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}			
			$scope.love_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('love_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.load_more_photos = function(){
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
				
			}	

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)
				$http.get('picture/' + picture_id + '/likes')
					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);
						$scope.individual_picture_likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}
		});
	</script>

	</body>
</html>

	<script>
		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload', 'infinite-scroll', 'ngMaterial', 'angularMoment' ]).
		config(function($sceProvider) {
			$sceProvider.enabled(false)
		}).
		controller('pixiController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
			}

			$scope.switch = function() {
				  	alert('hi');
				  	$scope = {
				    cb1: 'tdddddop',
				    cb1: 'ttt'
				  };

				  $scope.message = 'TOP 10';

				  $scope.onChange = function(cbState) {
				  	$scope.message = 'test';
				  };
			}

			$scope.upload_photo = function(file, error, next, $window) {
				console.log("inpicfunc" + $scope.upload.filename );
				if(!$scope.upload.filename) {
					console.log("no file found");
			   	
				} else {
					//console.log(file);
					Upload.upload({
	            	url: 'upload_photo/',
	            	method: 'POST',
	            	data: { 'file': $scope.upload.filename, 
	            			'title': $scope.upload.title, 
	            			'creator_id': $scope.user._id, 
	            			'username': $scope.user.email }
					}).then(function (resp) {
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.upload = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Photo Successfully Uploaded" },
			        	];
		        	
				    $scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
		        }, function (resp) {
		            console.log('Error status: ' + resp.status);
		        	});
				}
			}

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})
				
			}


			$scope.search = function(search_query){
				console.log($scope.search.query);
				$http.get('/search/?query=' + $scope.search.query)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
						$scope.search_response = null;
						$scope.clear_search = 1;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'success',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        ];
				        
				        }, function error(response) {
						$scope.search_response = response.data;
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'danger',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        	];
				       })
					}
				
			
			$scope.clear = function() {
				$scope.all_photos();
				$scope.clear_search = null;
				$scope.alerts = null;
			}

			$scope.user_info = function(){
				$http.get('user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})
				
			}
			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user_pictures = response.data;
					})
				
			}	
			$scope.like_photo = function(photo){
				console.log('in like');
				$http.get('like_photo/'+ photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}			
			$scope.love_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('love_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.load_more_photos = function(){
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
				
			}	

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)
				$http.get('picture/' + picture_id + '/likes')
					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);
						$scope.individual_picture_likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}
		});

	<script>
		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload', 'infinite-scroll', 'ngMaterial', 'angularMoment' ]).
		config(function($sceProvider) {
			$sceProvider.enabled(false)
		}).
		controller('pixiController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
			}

			$scope.switch = function() {
				  	alert('hi');
				  	$scope = {
				    cb1: 'tdddddop',
				    cb1: 'ttt'
				  };

				  $scope.message = 'TOP 10';

				  $scope.onChange = function(cbState) {
				  	$scope.message = 'test';
				  };
			}

			$scope.upload_photo = function(file, error, next, $window) {
				console.log("inpicfunc" + $scope.upload.filename );
				if(!$scope.upload.filename) {
					console.log("no file found");
			   	
				} else {
					//console.log(file);
					Upload.upload({
	            	url: 'upload_photo/',
	            	method: 'POST',
	            	data: { 'file': $scope.upload.filename, 
	            			'title': $scope.upload.title, 
	            			'creator_id': $scope.user._id, 
	            			'username': $scope.user.email }
					}).then(function (resp) {
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.upload = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Photo Successfully Uploaded" },
			        	];
		        	
				    $scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
		        }, function (resp) {
		            console.log('Error status: ' + resp.status);
		        	});
				}
			}

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})
				
			}


			$scope.search = function(search_query){
				console.log($scope.search.query);
				$http.get('/search/?query=' + $scope.search.query)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
						$scope.search_response = null;
						$scope.clear_search = 1;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'success',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        ];
				        
				        }, function error(response) {
						$scope.search_response = response.data;
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'danger',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        	];
				       })
					}
				
			
			$scope.clear = function() {
				$scope.all_photos();
				$scope.clear_search = null;
				$scope.alerts = null;
			}

			$scope.user_info = function(){
				$http.get('user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})
				
			}
			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user_pictures = response.data;
					})
				
			}	
			$scope.like_photo = function(photo){
				console.log('in like');
				$http.get('like_photo/'+ photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}			
			$scope.love_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('love_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.load_more_photos = function(){
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
				
			}	

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)
				$http.get('picture/' + picture_id + '/likes')
					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);
						$scope.individual_picture_likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}
		});
	</script>

	</body>
</html>

	<script>
		var app = angular.module('pixi',['ngSanitize', 'ngFileUpload', 'infinite-scroll', 'ngMaterial', 'angularMoment' ]).
		config(function($sceProvider) {
			$sceProvider.enabled(false)
		}).
		controller('pixiController', function($scope, $http, $sce, Upload){

			$scope.all_photos = function() {
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
			}

			$scope.switch = function() {
				  	alert('hi');
				  	$scope = {
				    cb1: 'tdddddop',
				    cb1: 'ttt'
				  };

				  $scope.message = 'TOP 10';

				  $scope.onChange = function(cbState) {
				  	$scope.message = 'test';
				  };
			}

			$scope.upload_photo = function(file, error, next, $window) {
				console.log("inpicfunc" + $scope.upload.filename );
				if(!$scope.upload.filename) {
					console.log("no file found");
			   	
				} else {
					//console.log(file);
					Upload.upload({
	            	url: 'upload_photo/',
	            	method: 'POST',
	            	data: { 'file': $scope.upload.filename, 
	            			'title': $scope.upload.title, 
	            			'creator_id': $scope.user._id, 
	            			'username': $scope.user.email }
					}).then(function (resp) {
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.upload = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
			        		{ type: 'success',  msg: "Photo Successfully Uploaded" },
			        	];
		        	
				    $scope.closeAlert = function(index) {
				        $scope.alerts.splice(index, 1);
				    }
		        }, function (resp) {
		            console.log('Error status: ' + resp.status);
		        	});
				}
			}

			$scope.all_users = function(){
				$http.get('all_users/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.registered_users = response.data;
					})
				
			}


			$scope.search = function(search_query){
				console.log($scope.search.query);
				$http.get('/search/?query=' + $scope.search.query)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.pictures = response.data;
						$scope.search_response = null;
						$scope.clear_search = 1;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'success',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        ];
				        
				        }, function error(response) {
						$scope.search_response = response.data;
						$scope.pictures = null;
						$scope.alerts = [
				        	{ type: 'danger',  msg: $scope.pictures.length + " photos found for " + $scope.search.query },
				        	];
				       })
					}
				
			
			$scope.clear = function() {
				$scope.all_photos();
				$scope.clear_search = null;
				$scope.alerts = null;
			}

			$scope.user_info = function(){
				$http.get('user_info/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user = response.data[0];
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_likes = function(){
				$http.get('user_likes/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}

			$scope.user_loves = function(){
				$http.get('user_loves/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.loves = response.data;
					})
				
			}
			$scope.user_pictures = function(){
				$http.get('user_pictures/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.user_pictures = response.data;
					})
				
			}	
			$scope.like_photo = function(photo){
				console.log('in like');
				$http.get('like_photo/'+ photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}			
			$scope.love_photo = function(photo){
				$http.get('love_photo/' + photo)
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.all_photos();
						$scope.user_info();
						$scope.user_likes();
						$scope.user_loves();
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.total_money = function(){
				$http.get('total_money/')
					.then(function success(response){
						$scope.total_money = response.data;
					})
				
			}	

			$scope.load_more_photos = function(){
				$http.get('pictures/')
				.then(function success(response) {
					$scope.pictures = response.data;
					//$scope.likes_count = $scope.pictures.likes.length;
				});
				
			}	

			$scope.picture_likes = function(picture_id){
				//console.log(picture_id)
				$http.get('picture/' + picture_id + '/likes')
					.then(function success(response){
						console.log(response.data);
						$scope.individual_picture_likes = response.data;
					})
				
			}
		});
	</script>

	</body>
</html>

          Solution

Appendix

Alert Types

This section contains additional information on the types of alerts in the report.

  1. Vulnerable JS Library

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Vulnerable JS Library (Powered by Retire.js))
    CWE ID 1395
    Reference
    1. https://owasp.org/Top10/A06_2021-Vulnerable_and_Outdated_Components/

  2. Absence of Anti-CSRF Tokens

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Absence of Anti-CSRF Tokens)
    CWE ID 352
    WASC ID 9
    Reference
    1. https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Cross-Site_Request_Forgery_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html
    2. https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/352.html

  3. CSP: Failure to Define Directive with No Fallback

    Source raised by a passive scanner (CSP)
    CWE ID 693
    WASC ID 15
    Reference
    1. https://www.w3.org/TR/CSP/
    2. https://caniuse.com/#search=content+security+policy
    3. https://content-security-policy.com/
    4. https://github.com/HtmlUnit/htmlunit-csp
    5. https://web.dev/articles/csp#resource-options

    Source raised by a passive scanner (Content Security Policy (CSP) Header Not Set)

    WASC ID 15
